HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000555/B003","root_000715/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_130e4a5f97f7015ced9a","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:5:2\",\"root_000555\",[\"B003\"]],[\"95:5:3\",\"root_000715\",[\"B002\"]],[\"95:5:4\",\"root_000715\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Geri çevirme, değeri ölçülmüş bir şeyin geçersiz ya da ayarı bozuk bulunup reddedilmesi gibi yargısal bir eleme anlamı da kazanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000555/B001","root_000715/B001","root_001273/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_228ddd0cf58eea7f3479","text":"1. **Downward return as reversal of upright taqwim.** Lexical evidence: ر د د B001, returning/sending back; س ف ل B001/B004, lowness and directing downward; ق و م B002/B008/B011 in 95:4, bodily uprightness/straightness/stature. Structural cue: ثم marks sequence from best taqwim to lowest low. Assigned role: رددناه supplies the reversal action; سفل supplies the spatial endpoint; ق و م supplies the prior upright state being reversed. Abductive move: I infer that \"return\" is not neutral motion but undoing of the previously established upright proportion. Reading change: the ayah reads as a deliberate inversion of formed height, not merely punishment vocabulary.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000555/B002","root_000555/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_2d5b5beecb93167d83b1","text":"95:6 immediately corrected any universal-endpoint reading: *illā* and the assent/action criteria made fixed `ر د د/B002` visible as a possible counter-vector that blocks the descent. Then 95:7–8 made fixed `ر د د/B003` newly salient: reckoning and adjudication turned the return into something like rejection after appraisal, while preserving the direct spatial descent as a separate reading.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000555/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_3468834c4597477ca71c","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:5:2\",\"root_000555\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"İstisna ayeti, geri çevirmenin engelleme ve geri püskürtme anlamını etkinleştirir; güven ve iyi işler aşağı yönün zorunlu son olmasını önleyen karşı bir güç olur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000555/B002","root_001449/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_48faf473c3051680d800","text":"4. **A downward vector that can be blocked.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ر د د/B002` supplies repelling, preventing, or turning something away. The next ayah’s *illā* excludes those marked by `ء م ن/B002`, `ع م ل/B001`, and `ص ل ح/B001`; its `م ن ن/B001` denies interruption of their reward. **Structural cue:** the grammatical exception immediately interrupts the general downward statement. **Assigned role:** the dormant repelling branch of the fixed verb becomes a counter-vector, while assent and sound action identify the contextual condition attached to it. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies exception but does not explicitly say deeds repel descent; I infer descent → blocked trajectory from the adjacency and exception syntax. **Reading change:** 95:5 establishes a real downward movement without making it an undifferentiated or universal endpoint.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000555/B010","root_000715/B001","root_000715/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_572317a7c6acca95aaa8","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:5:2\",\"root_000555\",[\"B010\"]],[\"95:5:3\",\"root_000715\",[\"B001\",\"B004\"]],[\"95:5:4\",\"root_000715\",[\"B001\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Tekrarlı geri çevirme ve iki kez vurgulanan aşağılık, düşüşü tek adımlı değil, katmanlı bir bükülme ve kararsızlık süreci olarak da gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000555/B009","root_000715/B005","root_000715/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_63fda3c4a5ab4932d45a","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:5:2\",\"root_000555\",[\"B009\"]],[\"95:5:3\",\"root_000715\",[\"B005\",\"B006\"]],[\"95:5:4\",\"root_000715\",[\"B005\",\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Destek ve alt uzuv anlamları, düşüşü insanı ayakta tutan dayanakların çözülmesiyle oluşan bedensel bir çöküş gibi okumayı mümkün kılar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000555/B003","root_000715/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_6443ef0766c594b174c7","text":"2. **Rejection after appraisal.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ر د د/B003` supplies refusing an invalid item and returning debased coins; fixed `س ف ل/B002` supplies lowered rank and baseness. The preceding `ق و م/B010` and `ق و م/B015` supply valuation and exact weight, while 95:6–8 supply work, recompense, reckoning, and adjudication. **Structural cue:** the return follows best valuation and is immediately qualified by an exception based on assent and sound action. **Assigned role:** *radd* acts as quality-control rejection, and *sufl* records the resulting downgrade. **Abductive move:** the packet does not state a failed test; I infer failed appraisal → return from the debased-coin branch and the later deed-based exception. **Reading change:** “We returned him” admits a judicial-economic reading in which endowed human worth is assessed and can be rejected as not matching its measure.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000148/B005","root_000555/B010","root_001290/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_771093a0f02a8df6adcb","text":"4. **Return as repetitive wavering into confusion.** Lexical evidence: ر د د B010, repetition, wavering, bewilderment; ب ل د B005, confusion/collapse; ك ذ ب B007, wild animal runs then stops to look back. Structural cue: 95:5 lies between measured creation and the \"what then makes you deny?\" question. Assigned role: radd can depict folding back and wavering, while later denial looks like a stop-start failure to proceed from evidence. Abductive move: I infer that descent may operate through repeated self-reversal rather than a single drop. Reading change: the ayah can describe a person turned back into oscillation and bewilderment after having been made upright.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000555/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_9238ca6dab4e0b011bad","text":"95:6 prevented 95:5 from closing as simple degradation: the exception made ر د د B003, rejection/invalidity, more precise than a merely spatial fall, because some are not left in the rejected state. 95:8 then activated restraint/judgment branches of ح ك م, so the downward return could be read as part of a governed process rather than uncontrolled collapse. The surprise is that \"lowest\" is not the last word; it creates the condition from which exception and adjudication become visible.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000715/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_bf139fb9352c08657e4b","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:5:3\",\"root_000715\",[\"B002\"]],[\"95:5:4\",\"root_000715\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"En aşağı oluş, yalnız mekânsal derinliği değil, insanın rütbe ve değer bakımından bayağılaşmasını da anlatır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000555/B010"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c4abf9dd66f541a691a9","text":"3. **Compounded rather than single-step reversal.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ر د د/B010` supplies repetition, wavering, and parts folded back upon one another. Fixed `س ف ل` occurs twice, as superlative location and plural inhabitant, while earlier `ط و ر/B004` supplies successive stages. **Structural cue:** the geminated/reduplicative shape of *raddadnāhu* is followed by the lexical doubling *asfala sāfilīn*. **Assigned role:** *radd* supplies recursive turning and the repeated low root intensifies the endpoint. **Abductive move:** the packet does not explicitly enumerate repeated descents; I infer that formal and lexical repetition make the reversal layered or compounded rather than instantaneous. **Reading change:** the ayah can portray a repeated folding-down of human setting, while leaving a single decisive return as a coexisting reading.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000148/B005","root_000504/B004","root_000715/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_c9e45601a56dfbf8a3fe","text":"3. **Lowest as social baseness, not only physical depth.** Lexical evidence: س ف ل B002, low rank and baseness; ب ل د B005/B007 in 95:3, confused collapse and dullness; د ي ن B004 in 95:7, humbling/subjugation. Structural cue: the descent occurs after the secure town and before the exception for believers/workers. Assigned role: سفل converts height/lowness into rank and moral/social baseness. Abductive move: I infer that the packet's vertical vocabulary becomes social-evaluative because faith/work/recompense immediately sort persons. Reading change: \"lowest of low\" is activated as degraded rank and condition, not only a place below.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B007","root_000555/B003","root_001290/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_cf7047ec4da66a13d36e","text":"2. **Rejection and invalidation of a corrupted form.** Lexical evidence: ر د د B003, rejection/returning as invalid; خ ل ق B007 in 95:4, fabricated false speech; ك ذ ب B002 in 95:7, declaring false. Structural cue: the fall is followed by an exception and then a denial-question. Assigned role: ردد can mean a form is sent back as unacceptable, like debased coin returned to a money changer. Abductive move: I infer that the \"lowest\" return can be a quality-control/judicial rejection when the human refuses the evidence. Reading change: the ayah reads as invalidation after failed valuation, not only a spatial descent.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000555/B009","root_000715/B005","root_001273/B009"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_e88f6d4eefa9292258bc","text":"5. **Loss of support beneath the upright body.** Lexical evidence: ر د د B009, prop/support preventing collapse; س ف ل B005/B006, lower limbs/offspring of camels; ق و م B009/B016, mainstay and stoppage/exhaustion. Structural cue: the word أسفل is doubled and follows taqwim, an upright body/support term. Assigned role: the lower parts and lost props make the fall bodily/mechanical: the support-system fails or is removed. Abductive move: I infer a structural engineering analogy from ق و م support to ر د د prop and س ف ل lower limbs. Reading change: the fall is not only moral demotion; it can be read as collapse of the supports that made standing possible.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000555/B001","root_000715/B001","root_000715/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_eb3c11fe130271ff043d","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:5:2\",\"root_000555\",[\"B001\"]],[\"95:5:3\",\"root_000715\",[\"B001\",\"B004\"]],[\"95:5:4\",\"root_000715\",[\"B001\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Aşağıya dönüş, önceki dimdik ve dengeli yapının hem bedensel hem de değer bakımından bilinçli biçimde tersine çevrilmesidir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000555/B001","root_000715/B001","root_001273/B008"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_f6b13c62582385aef353","text":"1. **Deliberate inversion of the created axis.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ر د د/B001` supplies returning someone to a former place or state; fixed `س ف ل/B001` supplies a place below height and `س ف ل/B004` directed descent. Prior `ق و م/B008` supplies straightness and uprightness, and `ق و م/B011` bodily stature (95:4). **Structural cue:** temporal *thumma*, the object pronoun referring back to the human, and the repeated root in *asfala sāfilīn* directly reverse the preceding “best *taqwīm*.” **Assigned role:** *radd* reverses state, while the two low forms specify both destination and class of destination. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies the two endpoints and an agentive return; I infer an axis upright → downturned on which the change is measured. **Reading change:** the ayah reads as a purposeful inversion of calibrated formation, not a vague statement of worsening.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
